```
def active_eg_refine(a, time_limit, t_start, top_q=20, eta0=0.05):
  a=a.copy(); S=1; best=score(a); no_improve=0
  while time:
    c=conv(a); M=c.max(); kmax...
    # active weights: top q lags with c > M - tol*M e.g. M*(1-1/(10q)) or top 5? 
    idx = np.argsort(c)[-top_q:]
    p=np.zeros(L); w=(c[idx]-M+W)? maybe exponential weights: w=exp(beta*(c[idx]-M)); normalize.
    cross=corr(p,a)
    grad = 2*n*(2*cross - 2*(p@c) a)  # for weighted average surrogate
    # x current? Use a multiplicative directly:
    # r = grad / a (clip) since grad sum ~0
    r = grad / (a+1e-300)
    for eta in candidates:
        a_new = a * np.exp(-eta*np.clip(r,-10,10))
        if any nan: ...
        S=a_new.sum(); if S<=0 continue
        a_new/=S
        sc=score(a_new)
        if sc<best* (1-1e-9) maybe ensure >0? improve:
           a=a_new; best=sc; ... break
    if no improve: reduce eta, reset candidates; maybe perturb.
```

Parameters for C_mod:
Given C (length L=2n-1), sum=1 (since f sum1). Desired upper bound M_target. We want C_new satisfying:

```
def spectral_refine(a, M_target, max_iter=100, alpha=0.7, time...):
   a=a.copy(); S=a.sum(); a/=S
   best=score(a); history=[]
   for it in range(max_iter):
      A=fft(a,N)
      C_hat=A*A
      C=ifft(C_hat).real[:L]
      M=c.max()
      if M <= M_target: break
      Cmod=make_bounded(C,M_target)
      Cmod_hat=fft(Cmod,N)
      # Newton A_new = 0.5*(A + Cmod_hat/A)
      den=A; absden=abs(den)
      new=np.zeros_like(A)
      mask=absden>1e-12
      new[mask]=0.5*(den[mask] + Cmod_hat[mask]/den[mask])
      # for not mask, use 0.5*Cmod_hat/den? no; maybe new = np.sqrt(Cmod_hat)*np.exp(0.5j*angle(den))?
      # Better: ratio = Cmod_hat / (den*den) where den not zero; new=den*exp(0.5*np.log(ratio)). Use mask.
      A2=den*den
      ratio=Cmod_hat/(A2+1e-30)
      # choose log branch close to den phase: new = den * exp(0.5*(log(Cmod_hat)-log(A2)))? Since log A2 = 2 log A + 2πik ambiguity. Newton avoids branch.
      new[0]=1.0
      g=ifft(new).real
      # choose window
      pos=np.maximum(g,0.0)
      # sliding sum length n over N (cyclic) maybe using prefix on 2*N
      start=best_window_start(pos,n,N)
      f_new = np.concatenate([g[start:start+n if <N else ...], ...])
      f_new=np.maximum(f_new,0.0)
      if f_new.sum()<1e-6: f_new=g[:n].clip(0)
      f_new/=f_new.sum()
      # blend
      f_blend=(1-alpha)*a + alpha*f_new
      f_blend=np.maximum(f_blend,0); f_blend/=f_blend.sum()
      sc=score(f_blend)
      if sc < best: a=f_blend; best=sc
      # optionally if no improve, reduce alpha or increase M_target? break
   return a,best
```

```
phaseA=np.angle(A_hat)
phaseC=np.angle(C_hat)
```

```
   xs = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   inits=[]
```

Let's estimate spectral iteration cost for n=2048: Full FFT N=4096: 5 FFTs ~0.5ms? Actually Python `np.fft.fft` 4096 complex ~0.05ms? Very fast. 1000 iter <0.5s. Could run thousands. Time dominated by Python loops and score evaluations. We can run many spectral iterations. Good.

We can run spectral from all initial shapes almost exhaustively within 2000s. This might find near-optimal.

Let's detail one spectral iteration with candidate blending and score:
```
def spectral_step(a, M_target, alpha_candidates=(1.0,0.8,0.6,0.4), window=True):
    N=fft_len_for_conv(n)
    A=np.fft.fft(a,N)
    C=np.fft.ifft(A*A).real[:L]
    C[C<0]=0
    S2=C.sum()
    # Scale C to sum1? if due numerical, C/=S2 maybe
    Cmod=make_bounded(C, M_target, S2=1)
    Chat=np.fft.fft(Cmod,N)
    mag=np.abs(Chat); phaseA=np.angle(A); phaseC=np.angle(Chat)
    delta = np.angle(np.exp(1j*(phaseC - 2*phaseA))) # or wrap via np.arctan2
    new_phase = phaseA + 0.5*delta
    new = np.sqrt(mag) * np.exp(1j*new_phase)
    new[0]=1.0
    g=np.fft.ifft(new).real
    # candidate projections:
    cands=[]
    # window with maximal positive sum
    pos=np.maximum(g,0)
    if n<N:
       pos2=np.concatenate([pos,pos])
       pref=np.concatenate([[0],np.cumsum(pos2)])
       sums=pref[n:n+N]-pref[:N]
       s=int(sums.argmax())
       win=pos2[s:s+n]  # already nonnegative
       cands.append(win)
    # maybe original first n positive, and folded positive
    cands.append(np.maximum(g[:n],0))
    if N> n:
       fold=pos[:n]+pos[n:]
       cands.append(fold)
    # also include maybe negative branch? If sum of g negative? new[0]=1 so sum positive.
    best_local=None
    for fnew in cands:
       if fnew.sum() <= 1e-8: continue
       fnew=fnew/fnew.sum()
       for alpha in alpha_candidates:
          blend=(1-alpha)*a + alpha*fnew
          blend=np.maximum(blend,0)
          if blend.sum()<=1e-8: continue
          blend/=blend.sum()
          sc, cmax = exact_score_blend? compute_score only
          if best is None or sc<best: best...
    return best_a,best_score
```
This may do up to 3*4=12 score evaluations per spectral step (each FFT self). Too much. But score eval cheap? Each score self FFT length4096, ~0.1ms; 12*1000=12k FFT ~1s. okay. But we can reduce: evaluate fnew (alpha=1) and one blend alpha=0.7,0.5. Compute score for candidates. 6 scores per iter. Fine.

We can cache C/max from step? Need exact score for blend; compute via `compute_score` FFT. Could use same FFT? Not easily. But okay.

To speed, in spectral_iter accept if fnew score < current_score. If not, try blends. We can just evaluate fnew first. If fnew improves, update; else try blends. This saves.

Since spectral step itself uses old A to compute C; if after blending we update a, next iteration recomputes.

```
no=0
for it in range(max_iter):

   else:
```

- Phase B: For top 8, allocate 100s spectral (or 500s total). Run spectral_multi from current best with progressively lower targets. Keep top 4.
- Phase C: LSE polish top 4 with log-space Adam for 100s each (400s). Starting from log(a). Beta gamma schedule. This refines.
- Phase D: Spectral again from LSE results for 200s to break LSE local minima.
- Phase E: Final LSE high beta 100s + active gradient 100s.
- Return best.

This is robust but code lengthy. Need keep within constraints no closures. Could be manageable.

Detailed helper definitions:
```

    _ = height_sequence_1
except NameError: height_sequence_1 = None
```

FFT sizes: We can compute each call; for n=2048, bit_length cheap. For full FFT in spectral, need N = next_pow2(2*n - 1). For self-LSE cross, M1=next pow 2n-1, M2=next pow 3n-2. We can pass to functions.

Helper:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
For full FFT of circular root, if 2n-1 exactly power? N=nextpow2(2n-1). For n=2048, 4095 ->4096. Good.

`compute_score(a)`:
```
def compute_score(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=len(a); S=a.sum()
    if S <= 1e-15: return np.inf
    a=a/S
    M=2*n-1
    N=next_pow2(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
    mx=float(c.max())
    return float(2.0*n*mx)  # since normalized S=1
```
This normalizes. For optimization, if a already sum1, same. Could return scale. To avoid normalizing if already, but okay.

`compute_score_raw(a)` maybe with S.

`conv_self(a,N=None)`:
```
def conv_self(a, N=None):
   n=len(a); M=2*n-1; if N is None N=next_pow2(M); A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]; return c
```

`cross_corr(p,a,N=None)`:
```
def cross_corr(p,a,N=None):
   n=len(a); L=len(p) # assume 2n-1
   p_rev=p[::-1]
   LC=L+n-1; if N is None N=next_pow2(LC)
   Pr=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,N); Ar=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(Pr*Ar,N)
   # return conv[L-1 : L-n-1 : -1]
   return conv[L-1 : L-n-1 : -1]
```
Check for n=1: L=1, stop=1-1-1=-1? `conv[0:-2:-1]` wrong. But n large. We can handle n==1 separately.

`softmax_log(x)`:
```
def softmax_log(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    xm=x - x.max()
    np.clip(xm, -745, 709, out=xm)
    a=np.exp(xm)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(len(x))/len(x)
    return a/s
```
`log_from_a(a)`:
```
def log_from_a(a):
   amin=np.max(1e-18, a.min()?)
   a2=np.maximum(a,1e-18)
   l=np.log(a2)
   return l - l.mean()
```

`lse_grad_log(x, beta, sizes)`:
We need beta absolute. We can use n. `a=softmax_log(x)`, c=conv_self(a,N1). `cmax=c.max()`. scaled=beta*(c-cmax). If beta maybe 0? p uniform. Use `p=np.exp(scaled)` (max 1) but if scaled very negative underflows okay; psum≥1. `L=cmax + math.log(psum)/beta`. `g=2.0*n*(2.0*cross_corr(p,a,N2) - 2.0*L*a)`. Wait cross_corr(p,a) uses p length 2n-1. For p normalized sum1. Then `mu=float(np.dot(a,g))`; `gx=a*(g-mu)`. Return F=2*n*L, exact=2*n*cmax, gx.
Need if beta extremely large, L approx cmax; p sum maybe ties; okay. But `math.log(0)` no because psum≥1.

Beta schedule absolute: For n, gamma values. `betas = [gamma*n for gamma in [1,5,20,100,500,2000,5000?]]`. But LSE with beta=5000*n huge; `scaled` difference Δc~1e-7, betaΔ=0.5? For n=2048 beta=10M, Δ1e-7=>1. Good. If diff smaller, one-hot.

Potential issue: c values O(1/n), c-cmax negative many. scaled may be large negative, exp underflow. Good.

`adam_log(x, betas, lrs, time_limit, t_start, check_interval=10)`:
```
def adam_log(x, betas, lrs, time_limit, t_start, max_step_per_beta=None):
    n=len(x); x=x.copy()
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); b1,b2,eps=0.9,0.999,1e-8
    a=softmax_log(x); best_s=compute_score(a)
    best_x=x.copy()
    # prepare sizes
    N1=next_pow2(2*n-1); N2=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    for beta,lr in zip(betas,lrs):
       t_global=0
       while time.monotonic() - t_start < time_limit:
          # compute f, exact, gx
          f, exact, gx = lse_grad_log(x,beta,N1,N2)
          t_global +=1
          m=b1*m+(1-b1)*gx; v=b2*v+(1-b2)*gx*gx
          th=m/(1-b1**t_global); vth=v/(1-b2**t_global)
          step = lr / (1+1e-5*t_global?)
          x = x - step*th/(np.sqrt(vth)+eps)
          # clip x and renormalize
          x=np.clip(x, -700, 700)
          if t_global%100==0: x=x-x.mean()
          if t_global%check==0:
             a=softmax_log(x); sc=compute_score(a)
             if sc<best_s: best_s=sc; best_x=x.copy(); best_a=a
       # end beta
    return best_a,best_score
```
This may be too unbounded: It will use all time for one candidate; caller should allocate per beta via time_limit total. If betas multiple, it loops all betas but each while condition false? Wait if time exceeds after first beta, it exits only while but then still loops next betas and while condition false immediately. It will return after full betas. Good. But if time left large, it spends it all on first beta, not schedule. We need allocate per beta. Better pass total time and split among betas: `time_per = time_limit/len(betas)`. For each beta, loop until t_start + bi*time_per or total. We'll implement with list and allocations. Or simply use one beta at a time in caller. Simpler: `adam_log_schedule(x, schedule, time_limit, t_start)` where schedule is list of (beta,lr); time_per=(time_limit-2)/len(schedule); for each, while time < t_start + allocated - small. Good.

`active_gradient_refine(a, time_limit, t_start)`:
We can implement exact active EG in log-space.
```
def active_refine(a, time_limit, t_start, q=8):
   n=len(a); a=a.copy(); best=score(a); x=log_from_a(a)
   N1=next_pow2(2n-1); N2=next_pow2(3n-2)
   eta=0.05
   last_improve=0
   it=0
   while time<t_start+limit:
       a=softmax_log(x)
       c=conv_self(a,N1); M=c.max(); L=2n-1
       # weights top q with exponential beta_active = max(10, q* n?) maybe
       beta_act=10.0*n  # but c small; differences M-c
       scaled=beta_act*(c-M)
       p=np.exp(scaled); psum=p.sum(); p=p/psum
       Lw=float(np.dot(p,c))
       cross=cross_corr(p,a,N2)
       g=2.0*n*(2.0*cross - 2.0*Lw*a)
       mu=a.dot(g); gx=a*(g-mu)
       # line search etas
       improved=False
       for e in [eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1]:
           xn=x - e*gx
           an=softmax_log(xn)
           sc=compute_score(an)
           if sc < best:
               x=xn; a=an; best=sc; eta=max(e*1.2,1e-4); improved=True; break
       if improved: last_improve=time.monotonic()
       else:
           eta*=0.5
           if time.monotonic()-last_improve>30: break
   return a,best
```
This may be too slow with compute_score each eta (1-2 FFT). But final 100s okay.
Could use q=1 or top exponential. For exact max, beta large. Use beta_act=20*n maybe.

Random perturbations final: Use log noise, Gaussian, peak-target. But maybe not needed.

Spectral functions in detail:
```
def make_bounded_conv(C, M_target, total=1.0):
   C=np.asarray(C,float).copy()
   C[C<0]=0
   s=C.sum()
   if s<=0: return C
   C=C*(total/s)
   avg=total/C.size
   if M_target < avg: M_target=avg*1.000001
   C=np.minimum(C, M_target)
   deficit=total-C.sum()
   if deficit <= 1e-14:
       return C
   head=M_target-C
   head_sum=head.sum()
   if head_sum <= 0:
       # scale flat
       return np.full_like(C, total/C.size) if feasible
   C += deficit*head/head_sum
   # Due rounding clip tiny
   C=np.clip(C,0,M_target)
   # renormalize? clip can reduce sum if addition pushed? but not.
   s=C.sum()
   if s>0: C*=total/s
   # might exceed M due renormalize if s<total? total/s=1 if sum. If s slightly less, max may exceed; handle by one more bounded pass? We can loop twice.
   return make_bounded_conv(C,M_target,total) maybe recursion
```
Avoid recursion; do loop 2.

`choose_window(g,n,N)`:
```
def best_window_positive(g,n,N):
   pos=np.maximum(g,0.0)
   if N==n: return pos.copy()
   pos2=np.concatenate([pos,pos])
   pref=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(pos2)))
   sums=pref[n:n+N]-pref[:N]
   s=int(np.argmax(sums))
   return pos2[s:s+n].copy()
```
If total pos maybe. Also maybe choose window maximizing sum of `g` not positive? If g mostly positive, same.

```
# Optionally zero out frequencies where mag<1e-14? no

   if fnew.sum()>1e-8:
```

`spectral_pass(a, M_target, time_limit, t_start, max_iter=2000, alpha_list=(1.0,0.7,0.5,0.3))`:
```
def spectral_pass(a, M_target, time_limit, t_start, max_iter=5000):
   a=a.copy(); S=a.sum(); a/=S
   best_a=a.copy(); best_s=compute_score(a)
   no_improve=0
   it=0
   deadline=t_start+time_limit
   while time.monotonic()<deadline and it<max_iter:
       it+=1
       cand,cand_s=spectral_project(a, M_target)
       # evaluate cand if cand_s maybe computed; cand may be None if all zero
       improved=False
       if cand is not None and cand_s < best_s:
           best_a=cand.copy(); best_s=cand_s; no_improve=0; improved=True
       else:
           # try blends of current a and cand
           for alp in (0.5,0.8,0.3,0.95):
               b=(1-alp)*a + alp*cand
               b=np.maximum(b,0)
               sb=b.sum()
               if sb<=1e-8: continue
               b/=sb
               sc=compute_score(b)
               if sc < best_s:
                   best_a=b; best_s=sc; a=b; no_improve=0; improved=True; break
           if not improved:
              # try small current? no
              no_improve += 1
              if no_improve > 50: break
       # If improved, set a=best_a for next iteration
       a=best_a
       # optional if best_s <= 2*M_target*n? (M_target achieved) break? But projection to M; score maybe below target.
       if best_s <= 2.0*n*M_target*1.0001:
           # achieved target; can break pass
           break
   return best_a,best_s
```
But `spectral_project` already scored cand. The pass blends score. Note if cand_s not computed because we didn't score? We'll score inside. Good.

Potential issue: In spectral_project, using `compute_score` normalizes a; if cand sum not 1. okay.

M_target for pass: Since if score=2n*cmax for normalized. If we set M_target = target_score/(2n). If best_s achieves <=target, break.

`spectral_multi(a, time_limit, t_start, target_floor=1.30, method_time=30, max_targets=50)`:
```
def spectral_multi(a, time_limit, t_start, target_floor=1.30, pass_time=20.0):
   a=a.copy(); S=a.sum(); if S: a/=S
   best=compute_score(a); n=len(a)
   target=best*0.98  # if best inf, set 1.8?
   if target < target_floor: target=target_floor
   target=max(target_floor, min(target, 1.8))
   deadline=t_start+time_limit
   plateau=0
   while time.monotonic()<deadline and target>target_floor+1e-4:
       M=target/(2.0*n)
       # if M < 1/(2n-1) set average
       minM=1.0/(2*n-1)
       if M<minM: M=minM
       # time remaining for pass
       rem=deadline - time.monotonic()
       if rem < 2: break
       # cap pass_time
       t_pass=min(pass_time, rem*0.6)
       new_a,new_s=spectral_pass(a,M,t_pass,time.monotonic())
       if new_s < best:
           a=new_a; best=new_s; plateau=0
           # if best < target, can try target lower by 1%
           target = max(target_floor, min(target, best*0.995))
       else:
           plateau +=1
           target = max(target_floor, target*0.985)
           if plateau>5: break
   # at end if time remains, maybe final pass with floor
   if time.monotonic()<deadline-2:
      M=target_floor/(2*n); new_a,new_s=spectral_pass(a,M,min(30,deadline-time.monotonic()),time.monotonic())
      if new_s<best: a,new_s...
   return a,best
```
This may spend too much if no improvement. Use pass_time e.g. 10s for initial. For top refine, pass_time 60s.

Maybe spectral_project from current a with M_target where M_target > current cmax? If target > best, no improvement? We set target lower than best; but current cmax = best/(2n). M_target lower. Good.

If current best=2, target floor1.3, many steps. Initial pass maybe improve gradually.

`resample_sequence(a, new_n)`:
```
def resample_sequence(a,new_n):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=len(a)
    if n==new_n: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.arange(n); x_new=np.linspace(0,n-1,new_n)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0)
    s=b.sum()
    if s>0: b/=s
    return b
```

Generate initials top-level:
```
def generate_initials(n, rng, parent_list):
    xs=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    inits=[]
    def add(v):
       v=np.asarray(v,float)
       v=np.maximum(v,1e-18)
       s=v.sum()
       if s>0: inits.append(v/s)
    # monomials
    for p in [0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,1.0,1.25,1.5,2.0,3.0]:
       add(xs**p); add((1-xs)**p)
    # linear combos
    for b in np.linspace(-1.9,1.9,13):
       add(1.0+b*xs)
    # quadratic skew
    for c in [-2.0,-1.0,1.0,2.0]:
       add(1.0 - 0.5*xs + 0.5*c*xs*(1-xs)? ensure positive)
    # piecewise linear
    for alpha in [0.25,0.5,0.75]:
       for u0 in [0.0,0.3,0.6,1.0]:
          for u1 in [0.4,1.0,1.6,2.2]:
             v=np.where(xs<alpha, u0+(u1-u0)*(xs/alpha), u1)
             add(v)
             v=np.where(xs<alpha, u0, u0+(u1-u0)*((xs-alpha)/(1-alpha))) # linear increasing? maybe
    # beta skew
    for aexp in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
       for bexp in [0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
           v=(xs+1e-6)**aexp * ((1-xs)+1e-6)**bexp
           add(v)
    # random fourier (10)
    for _ in range(10):
       modes=2+int(3*rng.random())
       base=1.0
       for m in range(1,modes+1):
           amp=rng.uniform(0.05,0.8)
           phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
           base += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*xs+phase)
       add(base)
    # random smooth full
    for _ in range(5):
       r=np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n))+0.2
       k=max(3,n//64)
       kk=np.ones(k)/k
       r=np.convolve(r, kk, mode='same')
       add(r)
    # parent
    if parent_list is not None:
       if len(parent_list)==n: add(parent_list)
       else: add(resample_sequence(parent_list,n))
       # symmetrized? Not pal, maybe add reversed parent
       add(parent_list[::-1])
    # fallback uniform
    add(np.ones(n))
    # deduplicate? not necessary but may waste.
    return inits
```

Let's write helper:
```
def adam_log_schedule(x, schedule, time_limit, t_start):
    n=len(x)
    N1=next_pow2(2*n-1); N2=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    a=softmax_log(x)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_s=compute_score(a,N1)
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t_global=0
    deadline=t_start+time_limit
    seg_time=(time_limit-0.5)/len(schedule)
    for idx in range(len(schedule)):
       beta,lr=schedule[idx]
       seg_deadline=t_start + (idx+1)*seg_time
       while time.monotonic() < min(deadline, seg_deadline):
           f, exact, gx = lse_grad_log(x,beta,N1,N2)
           t_global +=1
           m=...
           step=lr
           x = x - step*mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
           x=np.clip(x,-700,700)
           if t_global%1000==0: x-=x.mean()
           if t_global%20==0:
              a=softmax_log(x); sc=compute_score(a,N1)
              if sc<best_s: best_s=sc; best_a=a.copy()
    # final check
    a=softmax_log(x); sc=compute_score(a,N1); if sc<best_s: ...
    return best_a,best_s
```

Let's add explicit init `flat_root_init(n)`:
```
def flat_root_init(n):
    coeffs=np.empty(n)
    # recurrence to avoid huge numbers: c_m = binom(2m,m)/4^m
    c=1.0
    coeffs[0]=1.0
    for m in range(1,n):
       c *= (2*m -1? recurrence: C(2m,m)4^{-m} = C(2m-2,m-1)4^{-(m-1)} * ( (2m)(2m-1)/m^2 ) /4 = prev * ( (2m-1)/(2m) )
    because C(2m,m)=C(2m-2,m-1)*(2m)(2m-1)/m^2. Divide by4 => (2m-1)/(2m). So c *= (2m-1)/(2m) = (1 - 1/(2m)).
    coeffs[m]=c
    return coeffs
```
This decays ~1/sqrt(πm). Normalize. Include and reversed.

Let's write active_refine simpler:
```
def active_refine(a,time_limit,t_start, q=8, eta0=0.05):
    n=len(a); a=a.copy(); normalize
    x=log_from_a(a)
    N1=..., N2=...
    best=compute_score(a,N1)
    deadline=t_start+time_limit
    last_improve=time.monotonic()
    eta=eta0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and it<50000:
       it+=1
       a=softmax_log(x)
       c=conv_self(a,N1); M=c.max()
       if M <=0: break
       beta=20.0*n
       scaled=beta*(c-M)
       p=np.exp(scaled); psum=p.sum()
       if psum<=0: break
       p=p/psum
       L=float(np.dot(p,c))
       cross=cross_corr(p,a,N2)
       g=2.0*n*(2.0*cross - 2.0*L*a)
       mu=a.dot(g)
       gx=a*(g-mu)
       norm=np.linalg.norm(gx)
       if norm<1e-14: break
       best_eta=None
       for e in (eta, eta*0.5, eta*0.25, eta*0.1):
           xn=x - e*gx
           an=softmax_log(xn)
           sc=compute_score(an,N1)
           if sc < best - 1e-14:
              best_eta=e; best_sc=sc; break
       if best_eta is not None:
           x=x - best_eta*gx
           a=softmax_log(x)
           best=best_sc
           eta=min(0.5, max(1e-4, best_eta*1.2))
           last_improve=time.monotonic()
       else:
           eta*=0.5
           if time.monotonic()-last_improve > 20: break
    return a,best
```
Could be okay.

`make_bounded_conv` recursion? We'll implement loop:
```
def make_bounded_conv(C,M_target,total=1.0):
   C=np.asarray(C,float).copy()
   C[C<0]=0
   s=C.sum()
   if s<=0:
      C.fill(total/C.size); return C
   C *= total/s
   L=C.size; avg=total/L
   if M_target < avg: M_target=avg
   for _ in range(2):
      C=np.minimum(C,M_target)
      deficit=total-C.sum()
      if deficit <= 1e-14: break
      head=M_target-C
      head_sum=head.sum()
      if head_sum <= 0:
          return np.full(L,total/L) # infeasible? 
      C += deficit*head/head_sum
   C=np.clip(C,0,M_target)
   s=C.sum()
   if s>0: C *= total/s
   # If after renorm some exceed M due rounding, clip once and renormalize? Might loop infinite if M close avg. Do:
   C=np.minimum(C,M_target)
   s=C.sum()
   if s<total:
      # distribute a little
      head=M_target-C; hs=head.sum()
      if hs>0: C += (total-s)*head/hs
   return C
```
If M_target very close avg, head_sum small; addition may blow. But okay.

`spectral_project`: Before `make_bounded`, we compute C from A*A. Since A normalized sum1, C sum should 1. But if a sum1 after computed `C = ifft(A*A).real[:L]`. Due numerical negative. We can `C = np.maximum(C,0); ss=C.sum(); if ss>0: C/=ss` before make_bounded. Good.

One concern: Python `np.fft.fft` for full complex length N and `np.fft.ifft` in spectral_project; for N=4096 and many calls, okay. But in `spectral_project`, we call `compute_score_fft` which uses rfft. We could reuse `N` passed. We'll pass N to compute_score. For n1 2048, N self =4096; spectral full N =4096. Good. For n2 4096, N=8192.

`compute_score_fft`:
```
def compute_score_fft(a,N=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=len(a); S=a.sum()
   if S <= 1e-15: return np.inf
   if N is None: N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   # a normalized? Scale invariant, but for score if S not1, formula 2n max/c? If use a as is: c of a, M, score=2n*M/(S*S). We can avoid copying and normalizing maybe.
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
   mx=c.max()
   return float(2.0*n*mx/(S*S))
```
This is more efficient (no normalize copy). In LSE grad we need a normalized sum1. We'll have a from softmax sum1, but compute_score_fft works.

```python
"""
Hybrid search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(a*a) / (sum(a))**2
where a*a is the linear self-convolution.

The method combines:
  1. Structured initializations (power laws, flat-convolution square roots,
     parent sequence, Fourier/random smooth starts).
  2. A spectral projection iteration:
       - compute self-convolution C,
       - project C to a bounded/sum-preserving target,
       - take a phase-stable Fourier square root,
       - project back to a non-negative windowed sequence.
  3. Log-sum-exp smoothed gradient optimization in log-softmax variables.
  4. Active-set exact-gradient polish.

All helpers are top level. No lambdas/closures are used.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _resample_sequence(a, new_n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, new_n)
    if n == new_n:
        b = a.copy()
    else:
        x_old = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, n - 1.0, new_n)
        b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s > 1e-15:
        b = b / s
    else:
        b = np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / new_n
    return b


def _add_sequence(v, out):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s > 1e-12:
        v = v / s
        out.append(v.copy())


def _compute_score_fft(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.inf
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[: (2 * n - 1)]
    mx = float(c.max())
    if mx < 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (S * S))


def _conv_self(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    return c


def _cross_corr(p, a, N=None):
    """
    Return cross[j] = sum_i a[i] * p[i+j], j=0..n-1,
    where p has length 2*n-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    L = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(L + n - 1)
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    P = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, N)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    cv = np.fft.irfft(P * A, N)
    # Need cv[L-1], cv[L-2], ..., cv[L-n]
    return cv[L - 1 : L - n - 1 : -1]


def _softmax_log(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=700.0, neginf=-700.0)
    x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0)
    xm = x - float(x.max())
    a = np.exp(xm)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-30:
        return np.ones(len(x), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, len(x))
    return a / s


def _log_from_a(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-18)
    l = np.log(a)
    return l - float(l.mean())


def _flat_root_init(n):
    """
    Coefficients of the formal square root of a flat convolution sequence.
    c_k = 1/(2n-1), root coeff m = C(2m,m)/4^m.
    Recurrence: r_m = r_{m-1} * (2m-1)/(2m).
    """
    r = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cur = 1.0
    r[0] = 1.0
    for m in range(1, n):
        cur *= (2.0 * m - 1.0) / (2.0 * m)
        r[m] = cur
    s = float(r.sum())
    if s > 0.0:
        r /= s
    return r


def _generate_initials(n, rng, parent_list):
    out = []

    # Parent sequence, if useful.
    if parent_list is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent_list, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
        if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)) and p.sum() > 0:
            if p.size == n:
                _add_sequence(p, out)
                _add_sequence(p[::-1], out)
            else:
                pr = _resample_sequence(p, n)
                _add_sequence(pr, out)
                _add_sequence(pr[::-1], out)

    # Flat-convolution square-root initialization.
    fr = _flat_root_init(n)
    _add_sequence(fr, out)
    _add_sequence(fr[::-1], out)

    # Power-law/asymmetric families.  Alpha <= 0.5 is important for the
    # discrete endpoint coefficient not to dominate asymptotically.
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    j = n + 0.5 - i
    alphas = (0.45, 0.50)
    betas = (-0.10, -0.05, 0.0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35)
    for alpha in alphas:
        for beta in betas:
            v = (i ** (-alpha)) * (j ** (-beta))
            _add_sequence(v, out)

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)

    # Simple skewed linear / bent shapes.
    for b in (0.5, 1.2, 1.8, -0.5, -1.2):
        v = 1.0 + b * x
        _add_sequence(v, out)
    for c in (-1.5, -0.7, 0.7, 1.5):
        v = 1.0 + 0.5 * x + c * x * (1.0 - x)
        _add_sequence(v, out)

    # Piecewise linear shapes.
    for brk, lo, hi in ((0.25, 0.2, 1.8), (0.5, 0.1, 2.0), (0.75, 1.3, 2.6)):
        left = lo + (hi - lo) * (x / brk)
        right = hi + (lo - hi) * ((x - brk) / (1.0 - brk))
        v = np.where(x < brk, left, right)
        _add_sequence(v, out)

    # Fourier-modulated positive densities.
    for _ in range(6):
        base = 1.0
        modes = 2 + int(3 * rng.random())
        for m in range(1, modes + 1):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.8)
            phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            base += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + phase)
        _add_sequence(base, out)

    # Smooth random densities.
    for _ in range(4):
        r = np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)) + 0.25
        k = max(3, n // 80)
        kern = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / k
        r = np.convolve(r, kern, mode="same")
        _add_sequence(r, out)

    # Ensure at least some fallbacks.
    if not out:
        out.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n)

    # Trim to a manageable number.
    if len(out) > 28:
        out = out[:28]
    return out


def _lse_grad_log(x, beta, N1, N2):
    a = _softmax_log(x)
    n = len(a)
    c = _conv_self(a, N1)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        exact = np.inf
        return exact, exact, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    scaled = beta * (c - cmax)
    p = np.exp(scaled)
    psum = float(p.sum())
    if psum <= 1e-30:
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        idx = int(c.argmax())
        p[idx] = 1.0
        L = cmax
    else:
        p = p / psum
        L = cmax + math.log(psum) / beta

    # F(a) = 2*n*LSE(a) / sum(a)^2, on sum(a)=1 gives
    # grad = 2n*(2 cross(p,a) - 2 L a).
    cross = _cross_corr(p, a, N2)
    g = 2.0 * n * (2.0 * cross - 2.0 * L * a)
    mu = float(np.dot(a, g))
    gx = a * (g - mu)

    exact = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
    return exact, exact, gx


def _adam_log_schedule(x, schedule, time_limit, t_start):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = len(x)
    N1 = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    N2 = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    a0 = _softmax_log(x)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_s = _compute_score_fft(a0, N1)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b1, b2, eps = 0.9, 0.999, 1e-8
    t_global = 0
    deadline = t_start + float(time_limit)
    seg_time = max(1e-6, float(time_limit) / max(1, len(schedule)))

    for idx in range(len(schedule)):
        beta, lr0 = schedule[idx]
        lr_cur = float(lr0)
        seg_deadline = t_start + (idx + 1) * seg_time
        local_last_improve = time.monotonic()
        local_steps = 0

        while time.monotonic() < min(deadline, seg_deadline):
            ok = time.monotonic() < min(deadline, seg_deadline)
            if not ok:
                break
            exact, _, gx = _lse_grad_log(x, beta, N1, N2)
            local_steps += 1

            m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gx
            v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (gx * gx)
            t_global += 1
            mh = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t_global)
            vh = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t_global)

            x = x - lr_cur * mh / (np.sqrt(vh) + eps)
            x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 700.0)
            if t_global % 1000 == 0:
                x = x - float(x.mean())

            a = _softmax_log(x)
            sc = _compute_score_fft(a, N1)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-14:
                best_s = float(sc)
                best_a = a.copy()
                local_last_improve = time.monotonic()

            if time.monotonic() - local_last_improve > 25.0:
                lr_cur *= 0.65

    a = _softmax_log(x)
    sc = _compute_score_fft(a, N1)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
        best_s = float(sc)
        best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, best_s


def _make_bounded_conv(C, M_target, total=1.0):
    C = np.asarray(C, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    C[~np.isfinite(C)] = 0.0
    C[C < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = float(C.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        L = C.size
        val = total / float(L)
        return np.full(L, val, dtype=np.float64)
    C *= total / s
    L = C.size
    avg = total / float(L)
    if M_target < avg:
        M_target = avg * (1.0 + 1e-12)

    for _ in range(2):
        C = np.minimum(C, M_target)
        deficit = total - float(C.sum())
        if deficit <= 1e-14:
            break
        head = M_target - C
        head_sum = float(head.sum())
        if head_sum <= 1e-15:
            return np.full(L, total / L, dtype=np.float64)
        C += deficit * head / head_sum

    C = np.clip(C, 0.0, M_target)
    s = float(C.sum())
    if s > 0.0 and s !== total:
        C *= total / s
        C = np.minimum(C, M_target)
        deficit = total - float(C.sum())
        if deficit > 0.0:
            head = M_target - C
            head_sum = float(head.sum())
            if head_sum > 1e-15:
                C += deficit * head / head_sum
    return C


def _best_window_positive(g, n, N):
    pos = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    pos[~np.isfinite(pos)] = 0.0
    if N == n:
        return pos.copy()
    pos2 = np.concatenate((pos, pos))
    pref = np.empty(len(pos2) + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    pref[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(pos2, out=pref[1:])
    sums = pref[n : n + N] - pref[:N]
    s = int(np.argmax(sums))
    return pos2[s : s + n].copy()


def _spectral_project(a, M_target, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return None, np.inf
    a = a / S
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(L)

    A = np.fft.fft(a, N)
    Chat = A * A
    C = np.fft.ifft(Chat).real[:L]
    C[~np.isfinite(C)] = 0.0
    C[C < 0.0] = 0.0
    cs = float(C.sum())
    if cs > 0.0:
        C /= cs

    Cmod = _make_bounded_conv(C, M_target, total=1.0)
    Cmod_hat = np.fft.fft(Cmod, N)

    mag = np.abs(Cmod_hat)
    mag[~np.isfinite(mag)] = 0.0
    phaseA = np.angle(A)
    phaseC = np.angle(Cmod_hat)
    diff = phaseC - 2.0 * phaseA
    diff = (diff + np.pi) % (2.0 * np.pi) - np.pi

    new = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.complex128)
    mask = mag > 1e-18
    new_phase = phaseA + 0.5 * diff
    new[mask] = np.sqrt(mag[mask]) * np.exp(1j * new_phase[mask])
    # Lock DC to preserve sum=1.
    new[0] = 1.0

    g = np.fft.ifft(new).real
    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0

    cand1 = _best_window_positive(g, n, N)
    cand2 = np.maximum(g[:n], 0.0)
    cand3 = None
    if N > n:
        cand3 = np.maximum(g[:n], 0.0) + np.maximum(g[n:], 0.0)

    best_a = None
    best_s = np.inf
    for cand in (cand1, cand2, cand3):
        if cand is None:
            continue
        cs = float(cand.sum())
        if cs <= 1e-12:
            continue
        cand = cand / cs
        sc = _compute_score_fft(cand, N)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s:
            best_s = float(sc)
            best_a = cand.copy()

    return best_a, best_s


def _spectral_pass(a, M_target, time_limit, t_start, N=None, max_iter=100000):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return a, np.inf
    a = a / S
    n = len(a)
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _compute_score_fft(a, N)
    deadline = t_start + float(time_limit)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iter:
        it += 1
        cand, cand_s = _spectral_project(a, M_target, N)
        improved = False

        if cand is not None and np.isfinite(cand_s) and cand_s < best_s - 1e-14:
            best_a = cand.copy()
            best_s = float(cand_s)
            no_improve = 0
            improved = True

        if not improved and cand is not None:
            # Blend to avoid destructive clipping artifacts.
            for alpha in (0.75, 0.5, 0.95, 0.3):
                b = (1.0 - alpha) * best_a + alpha * cand
                b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
                bs = float(b.sum())
                if bs <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                b = b / bs
                sc = _compute_score_fft(b, N)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-14:
                    best_a = b.copy()
                    best_s = float(sc)
                    no_improve = 0
                    improved = True
                    break

        if not improved:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 45:
                break

        a = best_a

        # If convolution is already at or below target, stop this pass.
        if best_s <= 2.0 * n * M_target * 1.0005:
            break

    return best_a, best_s


def _spectral_multi(a, time_limit, t_start, target_floor=1.32, pass_time=10.0, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return a, np.inf
    a = a / S
    n = len(a)
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    best_s = _compute_score_fft(a, N)
    deadline = t_start + float(time_limit)

    # If current score is not finite, set a conservative target.
    if not np.isfinite(best_s) or best_s <= 1.1:
        return a, best_s

    target = max(target_floor, min(best_s * 0.985, 1.9))
    target = max(target_floor, target)
    plateau = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.0 and target > target_floor + 1e-5:
        M_target = target / (2.0 * n)
        minM = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)
        if M_target < minM:
            M_target = minM * 1.000001

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 2.0:
            break
        t_pass = min(float(pass_time), rem * 0.8)
        if t_pass < 1.0:
            break

        new_a, new_s = _spectral_pass(a, M_target, t_pass, time.monotonic(), N=N)
        if np.isfinite(new_s) and new_s < best_s - 1e-14:
            a = new_a.copy()
            best_s = float(new_s)
            plateau = 0
            target = max(target_floor, min(target, best_s * 0.995))
        else:
            plateau += 1
            target = max(target_floor, target * 0.985)
            if plateau >= 4:
                break

    # Final push to floor if time remains.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 3.0:
        M_target = max(target_floor / (2.0 * n), 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1))
        new_a, new_s = _spectral_pass(a, M_target, min(0.5 * rem + 1.0, 40.0), time.monotonic(), N=N)
        if np.isfinite(new_s) and new_s < best_s - 1e-14:
            a = new_a.copy()
            best_s = float(new_s)

    return a, best_s


def _active_refine(a, time_limit, t_start):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = len(a)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return a, np.inf
    a = a / S

    N1 = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    N2 = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    x = _log_from_a(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _compute_score_fft(a, N1)

    deadline = t_start + float(time_limit)
    last_improve = time.monotonic()
    eta = 0.04
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < 100000:
        it += 1
        a = _softmax_log(x)
        c = _conv_self(a, N1)
        M = float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        # Active-set weights: emphasize peaks, but keep a few near-peaks.
        beta = 25.0 * n
        scaled = beta * (c - M)
        p = np.exp(scaled)
        psum = float(p.sum())
        if psum <= 1e-30:
            p = np.zeros_like(c)
            p[int(c.argmax())] = 1.0
        else:
            p /= psum

        Lw = float(np.dot(p, c))
        cross = _cross_corr(p, a, N2)
        g = 2.0 * n * (2.0 * cross - 2.0 * Lw * a)
        mu = float(np.dot(a, g))
        gx = a * (g - mu)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(gx))
        if gnorm < 1e-15:
            break

        improved = False
        for e in (eta, 0.5 * eta, 0.25 * eta, 0.1 * eta):
            xn = x - e * gx
            an = _softmax_log(xn)
            sc = _compute_score_fft(an, N1)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s - 1e-14:
                x = xn
                a = an
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_s = float(sc)
                eta = min(0.25, max(1e-4, e * 1.25))
                improved = True
                last_improve = time.monotonic()
                break

        if not improved:
            eta *= 0.5
            if time.monotonic() - last_improve > 20.0:
                break

    return best_a, best_s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    """
    Entry point.  Returns a Python list of non-negative floats.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    final_deadline = t0 + budget + 10.0

    # Load previous best if available.
    parent_list = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
                if p.sum() > 0.0:
                    parent_list = p
    except Exception:
        parent_list = None

    # Main working length.  2048 gives a good accuracy/time trade-off.
    n1 = 2048
    if budget < 500.0:
        n1 = 1024

    N1 = _next_pow2(2 * n1 - 1)

    cands = []
    cand_id = 0

    def no_l_placeholder():
        pass

    # Candidate helper without closure: inline appending below.

    # Add parent at its own length if not too big.
    if parent_list is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent_list, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
        if 32 <= p.size <= 8192 and p.sum() > 0.0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            pn = p / float(p.sum())
            s = _compute_score_fft(pn)
            if np.isfinite(s):
                cands.append((float(s), cand_id, pn.copy()))
                cand_id += 1

    # Generate n1-sized inits and add their raw scores as fallbacks.
    initials = _generate_initials(n1, rng, parent_list)
    for a in initials:
        s = _compute_score_fft(a, N1)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            cands.append((float(s), cand_id, a.copy()))
            cand_id += 1

    def sort_candidates():
        cands.sort()

    def get_best():
        sort_candidates()
        if not cands:
            return None, np.inf
        return cands[0][2], cands[0][0]

    def add_candidate(a, N_use=None):
        nonlocal cand_id
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s > 0.0:
            a = a / s
        sc = _compute_score_fft(a, N_use)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            cands.append((float(sc), cand_id, a.copy()))
            cand_id += 1

    # Phase A: spectral screening of many structured starts.
    phaseA_end = min(t0 + 0.28 * budget, final_deadline - 120.0)
    maxA = min(24, len(initials))
    for idx in range(maxA):
        if time.monotonic() >= phaseA_end:
            break
        a0 = initials[idx].copy()
        rem = phaseA_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 3.0:
            break
        alloc = min(22.0, rem * 0.90)
        a1, s1 = _spectral_multi(a0, alloc, time.monotonic(),
                                 target_floor=1.45, pass_time=min(5.0, alloc / 3.5),
                                 N=N1)
        add_candidate(a1, N1)

    # Phase B: longer spectral refinement on best starts.
    get_best()
    top_b = [cands[i][2] for i in range(min(6, len(cands)))]
    phaseB_time = 0.20 * budget
    phaseB_end = min(t0 + 0.28 * budget + phaseB_time, final_deadline - 100.0)
    usedB = phaseB_end - phaseA_end
    if usedB < 10.0:
        usedB = max(10.0, 0.10 * budget)
        phaseB_end = min(phaseB_end, t0 + 0.28 * budget + usedB)
    perB = max(8.0, usedB / max(1, len(top_b)))
    for a0 in top_b:
        if time.monotonic() >= phaseB_end:
            break
        rem = phaseB_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 5.0:
            break
        alloc = min(perB, rem * 0.95)
        a1, s1 = _spectral_multi(a0.copy(), alloc, time.monotonic(),
                                 target_floor=1.34, pass_time=min(12.0, alloc / 4.0),
                                 N=N1)
        add_candidate(a1, N1)

    # Phase C: LSE-gradient polish on the best candidates.
    get_best()
    top_c = [cands[i][2] for i in range(min(5, len(cands)))]
    phaseC_time = 0.13 * budget
    phaseC_end = min(phaseB_end + phaseC_time, final_deadline - 90.0)
    usedC = phaseC_end - max(phaseB_end, time.monotonic())
    if usedC < 5.0:
        usedC = 0.08 * budget
        phaseC_end = min(time.monotonic() + usedC, final_deadline - 60.0)
    perC = max(6.0, usedC / max(1, len(top_c)))
    lse_schedule = []
    for gamma, lr in ((2.0, 0.10), (8.0, 0.07), (30.0, 0.045), (120.0, 0.025), (500.0, 0.012), (1500.0, 0.006)):
        lse_schedule.append((gamma * n1, lr))

    for a0 in top_c:
        if time.monotonic() >= phaseC_end:
            break
        rem = phaseC_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 4.0:
            break
        alloc = min(perC, rem * 0.95)
        if alloc < 3.0:
            break
        x0 = _log_from_a(a0)
        a1, s1 = _adam_log_schedule(x0, lse_schedule, alloc, time.monotonic())
        add_candidate(a1, N1)

        # Small perturbations + short polish can escape shallow minima.
        if perC > 10.0 and time.monotonic() < phaseC_end - 5.0:
            x0p = _log_from_a(a0) + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n1)
            ap, sp = _adam_log_schedule(x0p, lse_schedule[:4], max(4.0, alloc * 0.35), time.monotonic())
            add_candidate(ap, N1)

    # Phase D: spectral after LSE, to break LSE local minima.
    get_best()
    top_d = [cands[i][2] for i in range(min(4, len(cands)))]
    phaseD_time = 0.13 * budget
    phaseD_end = min(phaseC_end + phaseD_time, final_deadline - 80.0)
    usedD = phaseD_end - max(phaseC_end, time.monotonic())
    if usedD < 5.0:
        usedD = 0.08 * budget
        phaseD_end = min(time.monotonic() + usedD, final_deadline - 40.0)
    perD = max(8.0, usedD / max(1, len(top_d)))
    for a0 in top_d:
        if time.monotonic() >= phaseD_end:
            break
        rem = phaseD_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 5.0:
            break
        alloc = min(perD, rem * 0.95)
        a1, s1 = _spectral_multi(a0.copy(), alloc, time.monotonic(),
                                 target_floor=1.32, pass_time=min(12.0, alloc / 4.0),
                                 N=N1)
        add_candidate(a1, N1)

    # Phase E: upscale final best to 4096, if budget allows, and refine.
    n2 = 4096
    best_a, best_s = get_best()
    if best_a is not None and (final_deadline - time.monotonic()) > 160.0 and max(1, n1) < n2:
        N2 = _next_pow2(2 * n2 - 1)
        a2 = _resample_sequence(best_a, n2)
        add_candidate(a2, N2)

        phaseE_end = min(final_deadline - 70.0, time.monotonic() + 0.14 * budget)
        rem = phaseE_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 20.0:
            a3, s3 = _spectral_multi(a2.copy(), min(0.5 * rem + 1.0, 0.08 * budget),
                                     time.monotonic(), target_floor=1.31,
                                     pass_time=10.0, N=N2)
            add_candidate(a3, N2)

        rem = phaseE_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 15.0:
            lse_schedule2 = []
            for gamma, lr in ((4.0, 0.08), (15.0, 0.05), (60.0, 0.03), (250.0, 0.015), (1000.0, 0.008)):
                lse_schedule2.append((gamma * n2, lr))
            x2 = _log_from_a(a3 if best_a is not None else a2)
            a4, s4 = _adam_log_schedule(x2, lse_schedule2, min(0.4 * rem + 1.0, 0.06 * budget), time.monotonic())
            add_candidate(a4, N2)

    # Phase F: final exact active-gradient polish.
    if (final_deadline - time.monotonic()) > 25.0:
        best_a, best_s = get_best()
        if best_a is not None:
            n = len(best_a)
            ff = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
            rem = max(1.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic() - 8.0)
            a5, s5 = _active_refine(best_a.copy(), min(rem, 0.10 * budget), time.monotonic())
            add_candidate(a5, ff)

    # Choose overall best returned sequence.
    best_a, best_s = get_best()

    # Fallback uniform sequence if something unexpected happened.
    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_s):
        best_a = np.ones(n1, dtype=np.float64) / n1
        best_s = _compute_score_fft(best_a)

    # Ensure valid output under evaluate_sequence clamps.
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_a = np.nan_to_num(best_a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(best_a.sum())
    if S < 0.05:
        # Scale up to a safe sum but keep max under 1000.
        if max(1, len(best_a)) * 1000.0 < 1.0:
            best_a = best_a / max(S, 1e-300)
        else:
            best_a = np.ones(len(best_a), dtype=np.float64) / len(best_a)

    # Final internal verification.
    verified = _compute_score_fft(best_a)
    if not np.isfinite(verified):
        verified = float("inf")

    # Optional call to official evaluator for logging.  The sequence is already
    # in valid clamped range, so this should agree with _compute_score_fft.
    try:
        official = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a]))
        if np.isfinite(official):
            verified = min(verified, official)
            print(f"Final official score: {official:.10f}, internal: {verified:.10f}, n={len(best_a)}, time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")
    except Exception:
        print(f"Final internal score: {verified:.10f}, n={len(best_a)}, time={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s")

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
    return out
```
